Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the complexity structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 602. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 926 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by complexity generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 602, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 926. QED.
